**Requirements for TABs**

<table>
<tbody>
<tr><th>Competence</th><th>Description of competence</th><th>Requirement</th></tr>
<tr><td>1.<br>Analysing risks</td><td>Identify the possible risks and benefits for the use of innovative construction products in the absence of established/consolidated technical information regarding their performance when installed in construction works.</td><td rowspan="3">A TAB shall be established under national law and have legal personality. It shall be independent from the stakeholders and from any particular interests.<br>In addition, a TAB shall have staff with:<br>((a)) objectivity and sound technical judgement;<br>((b)) detailed knowledge of the regulatory provisions and other requirements in force in Great Britain, concerning product areas for which it is to be designated;<br>((c)) general understanding of construction practice and detailed technical knowledge, concerning product areas for which it is to be designated;<br>((d)) detailed knowledge of specific risks involved and the technical aspects of the construction process;<br>((e)) detailed knowledge of the existing designated standards and test methods within the product areas for which it is to be designated;<br>((f)) appropriate linguistic skills.<br>The remuneration of the TAB personnel shall not depend on the number of the assessments carried out or on the results of such assessments.</td></tr>
<tr><td>2.<br>Setting technical criteria</td><td>Transform the outcome of the risk analysis into technical criteria for evaluating behaviour and performance of the construction products regarding the fulfilment of applicable national requirements;<br>provide the technical information needed by those participating in the building process as potential users of the construction products (manufacturers, designers, contractors, installers).</td></tr>
<tr><td>3.<br>Setting assessment methods</td><td>Design and validate appropriate methods (tests or calculations) to assess performance for essential characteristics of construction products, taking into account the current state of the article.</td></tr>
<tr><td>4.<br>Determining the specific factory production control</td><td>Understand and evaluate the manufacturing process of the specific product in order to identify appropriate measures ensuring product constancy through the given manufacturing process.</td><td>A TAB shall have staff with appropriate knowledge of the relationship between the manufacturing processes and product characteristics related to factory production control.</td></tr>
<tr><td>5.<br>Assessing the product</td><td>Assess the performance for essential characteristics of construction products on the basis of harmonised methods against harmonised criteria.</td><td>In addition to the requirements listed in points 1, 2 and 3, a TAB shall have access to the necessary means and equipment for the assessment of the performance for essential characteristics of construction products within the product areas for which it is to be designated.</td></tr>
<tr><td>6.<br>General management</td><td>Ensure consistency, reliability, objectivity and traceability through the constant application of appropriate management methods.</td><td>A TAB shall have:<br>((a)) a proven record of respect of good administrative behaviour;<br>((b)) a policy and the supporting procedures to ensure confidentiality of sensitive information within the TAB and all its partners;<br>((c)) a document control system to ensure registration, traceability, maintenance and archiving of all relevant documents;<br>((d)) a mechanism for internal audit and management review to ensure the regular monitoring of the compliance with appropriate management methods;<br>((e)) a procedure to deal objectively with appeals and complaints.</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

[^c-3252404966f7d1cd661a2cb5f0409da3][^c-7aa43b76db5b4b9f3e5700413aaa5709]

## ANNEX V — ASSESSMENT AND VERIFICATION OF CONSTANCY OF PERFORMANCE[^c000002]

1\.

SYSTEMS OF ASSESSMENT AND VERIFICATION OF CONSTANCY OF PERFORMANCE

The manufacturer shall draw up the declaration of performance and determine the product-type on the basis of the assessments and verifications of constancy of performance carried out under the following systems:

1.1.

**System 1+**

- ((a)) The manufacturer shall carry out:
  - ((i)) factory production control;
  - ((ii)) further testing of samples taken at the manufacturing plant by the manufacturer in accordance with the prescribed test plan.
- ((b)) The approved[^c-40188a3ef8d95ccda5288b076ecdf720] product certification body shall decide on the issuing, restriction, suspension or withdrawal of the certificate of constancy of performance of the construction product on the basis of the outcome of the following assessments and verifications carried out by that body:
  - ((i)) an assessment of the performance of the construction product carried out on the basis of testing (including sampling), calculation, tabulated values or descriptive documentation of the product;
  - ((ii)) initial inspection of the manufacturing plant and of factory production control;
  - ((iii)) continuing surveillance, assessment and evaluation of factory production control;
  - ((iv)) audit — testing of samples taken by the approved product certification body at the manufacturing plant or at the manufacturer's storage facilities.

1.2.

**System 1**

- ((a)) The manufacturer shall carry out:
  - ((i)) factory production control;
  - ((ii)) further testing of samples taken at the manufacturing plant by the manufacturer in accordance with the prescribed test plan.
- ((b)) The approved product certification body shall decide on the issuing, restriction, suspension or withdrawal of the certificate of constancy of performance of the construction product on the basis of the outcome of the following assessments and verifications carried out by that body:
  - ((i)) an assessment of the performance of the construction product carried out on the basis of testing (including sampling), calculation, tabulated values or descriptive documentation of the product;
  - ((ii)) initial inspection of the manufacturing plant and of factory production control;
  - ((iii)) continuing surveillance, assessment and evaluation of factory production control.

1.3.

**System 2+**

- ((a)) The manufacturer shall carry out:
  - ((i)) an assessment of the performance of the construction product on the basis of testing (including sampling), calculation, tabulated values or descriptive documentation of that product;
  - ((ii)) factory production control;
  - ((iii)) testing of samples taken at the manufacturing plant by the manufacturer in accordance with the prescribed test plan.
- ((b)) The approved factory production control certification body shall decide on the issuing, restriction, suspension or withdrawal of the certificate of conformity of the factory production control on the basis of the outcome of the following assessments and verifications carried out by that body:
  - ((i)) initial inspection of the manufacturing plant and of factory production control;
  - ((ii)) continuing surveillance, assessment and evaluation of factory production control.

1.4.

**System 3**

- ((a)) The manufacturer shall carry out factory production control.
- ((b)) The approved laboratory shall assess the performance on the basis of testing (based on sampling carried out by the manufacturer), calculation, tabulated values or descriptive documentation of the construction product.

1.5.

**System 4**

- ((a)) The manufacturer shall carry out:
  - ((i)) an assessment of the performance of the construction product on the basis of testing, calculation, tabulated values or descriptive documentation of that product;
  - ((ii)) factory production control.
- ((b)) No tasks require the intervention of approved bodies.

1.6.

**Construction products for which a UK[^c-d43561b2b2cab673e819d76dbe9f39de] Technical Assessment has been issued**

Approved[^c-330753879188ca1a29115eb773f8908b] bodies undertaking tasks under Systems 1+, 1 and 3 as well as manufacturers undertaking tasks under Systems 2+ and 4 shall consider the UK Technical Assessment issued for the construction product in question as the assessment of the performance of that product. Approved bodies and manufacturers shall therefore not undertake the tasks referred to in points 1.1.(b)(i), 1.2.(b)(i), 1.3.(a)(i), 1.4.(b) and 1.5.(a)(i) respectively.

2\.

BODIES INVOLVED IN THE ASSESSMENT AND VERIFICATION OF CONSTANCY OF PERFORMANCE

With respect to the function of approved[^c-deefbdce0c220205372f9782a65d3af2] bodies involved in the assessment and verification of constancy of performance for construction products, distinction shall be made between:

- ((1)) product certification body: a body approved, in accordance with Chapter VII, to carry out constancy of performance certification;
- ((2)) factory production control certification body: a body approved, in accordance with Chapter VII, to carry out factory production control certification;
- ((3)) laboratory: a body approved, in accordance with Chapter VII, to measure, examine, test, calculate or otherwise assess the performance of construction products.

3\.

HORIZONTAL APPROVALS[^c-9c30fe2977606c4181c687379398b239]: CASES OF ESSENTIAL CHARACTERISTICS WHERE REFERENCE TO A RELEVANT HARMONISED TECHNICAL SPECIFICATION IS NOT REQUIRED

- 1.. Reaction to fire
- 2.. Resistance to fire
- 3.. External fire performance
- 4.. Acoustic performance
- 5.. Emissions of dangerous substances.
